      >                              vce(cluster pid)                                
      >         ///
      >                              winitial(unadjusted, independent)               
      >         ///
      >                              onestep                                         
      >         ///
      >                              from(`initial')                                 
      >         ///
      >                              quickderivatives nolog                          
      >         ///
      >                              iterate(`iterate')
       19. 
   665 .     local mu_test ""
       20.     local s0 : word 1 of $switchers
       21.     local mu_test "[mu]switcher_`s0'"
       22.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       23.         if `s' != `s0' {
       24.             local mu_test "`mu_test' = [mu]switcher_`s'"
       25.         }
       26.     }
       27.     test `mu_test'
       28.     estadd scalar joint_chi2 = r(chi2), replace : `estname'
       29.     estadd scalar joint_p    = r(p),    replace : `estname'
       30. 
   666 .     capture estat overid
       31.     if _rc == 0 {
       32.         estadd sca Jstat = r(J),    replace : `estname'
       33.         estadd sca Jdf   = r(J_df), replace : `estname'
       34.         estadd sca Jpval = r(J_p),  replace : `estname'
       35.     }
       36.     else {
       37.         di as text "run_grc_onestep: estat overid unavailable (onestep GMM
      > ) -- Jstat not computed"
       38.     }
       39.     local converged_str = cond(e(converged)==1, "Y", "N")
       40.     estadd local converged_str "`converged_str'", replace : `estname'
       41. 
   667 .     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'", replace
       42. 
   668 .     estimates restore `estname'
       43.     nlcom (Delta_never: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * ///
      >             (_b[mu:never] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post
       44.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_never", replace
       45. 
   669 .     estimates restore `estname'
       46.     nlcom (Delta_always: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] *  ///
      >             (_b[kappa:_cons] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post
       47.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_always", replace
       48. 
   670 .     estimates restore `estname'
       49.     local nlcom_expr ""
       50.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       51.         local nlcom_expr "`nlcom_expr' (Delta_`s': _b[Delta_base:_cons] + 
      > (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base'])))"
       52.     }
       53.     nlcom `nlcom_expr', post
       54.     local d_test ""
       55.     local s0 : word 1 of $switchers
       56.     local d_test "Delta_`s0'"
       57.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       58.         if `s' != `s0' {
       59.             local d_test "`d_test' = Delta_`s'"
       60.         }
       61.     }
       62.     test `d_test'
       63.     estadd scalar joint_chi2 = r(chi2), replace
       64.     estadd scalar joint_p    = r(p),    replace
       65.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_delta", replace
       66. 
   671 .     local first_loop = 1
       67.     local Delta_avg_nlcom ""
       68.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       69.         estimates restore `estname'
       70.         sum 1.switcher_`s' if e(sample)
       71.         local num_`s' = r(mean)
       72.         if `first_loop' == 0 {
       73.             local Delta_avg_nlcom "`Delta_avg_nlcom' + (`num_`s'' * (_b[De
      > lta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`bas
      > e']))))"
       74.         }
       75.         else if `first_loop' == 1 {
       76.             local Delta_avg_nlcom "(`num_`s'' * (_b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_
      > b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))))"
       77.             local first_loop = 0
       78.         }
       79.     }
       80.     estimates restore `estname'
       81.     nlcom (Delta_avg: `Delta_avg_nlcom'), post
       82.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_avg", replace
       83. 
   672 . end
      
   673 . 
   674 . * **********************************************************************
   675 . * GMM regression
   676 . * **********************************************************************
   677 . capture program drop run_grc_hukou
      
   678 . program define run_grc_hukou
        1. 
   679 .     * Syntax to accept user-specified covariates and estname
   680 .     syntax , estname(string) switchers(numlist) base(numlist)  balance(string
      > ) [covars(varlist) iterate(numlist) initial(string)] 
        2. 
   681 .     * Construct the covariates string for the regression and instruments
   682 .                 if "`balance'" == "unb" {
        3.                         local covarlist "`covars' unbalanced unbalanced_ch
      > oice"
        4.                 }
        5.                 else {
        6.                         capture drop covar_cons
        7.                         gen covar_cons = 0
        8.                         local covarlist "`covars' covar_cons"
        9.                 }
       10. 
   683 .                 * Initialize a local to hold switcher variables
   684 .                 local switcher_traj
       11.                 
   685 .                 * Loop through switchers and add them to local
   686 .                 foreach s of numlist `switchers' {
       12.                         local switcher_traj "`switcher_traj' switcher_`s'"
       13.                 }
       14.                 
   687 .                 * Build switcherpars internally — guarantees same base everyw
      > here
   688 .                 define_switcherpars, switchers(`switchers') base(`base')
       15.                 local switcherpars `r(switcherpars)'
       16.                 di as text "run_grc: base trajectory = `base'"
       17.                 
   689 .     * Run the GMM estimation
   690 .     eststo `estname': gmm (lndepvar - {mu: never `switcher_traj'}            
      >            ///
      >                                                                         - {De
      > lta_base}*choice                                                             
      >                                                               ///
      >                                                                         - {ph
      > i=-1}*(`switcherpars')                                                       
      >                                                       ///
      >                                                                         - ({k
      > appa}+{phi}*({kappa}                                                         
      >                               ///
      >                                                                         - {mu
      > : switcher_`base'}))*(always#1.choice)           ///
      >                                                                         - {xb
      > : `covarlist'})                                                              
      >                                                               ///
      >                                                                         , ins
      > truments(                                                                    
      >                                                                              
      >          ///
      >                                                                         `cova
      > rlist'                                                                       
      >                                                                              
      >                  ///
      >                                                                         never
      >  `switcher_traj' choice                                                      
      >                                               ///
      >                                                                         alway
      > s_choice switcher_*_choice, nocons                                           
      >               ///
      >                                                                         )    
      >                                                                              
      >                                                                              
      >                                                  ///
      >                       vce(cluster pid)                                       
      >                                                                              
      >               ///
      >                                                                              
      >            from(`initial')                                                   
      >                                                                              
      >          ///
      >                                                                              
      >            quickderivatives nolog                                            
      >               ///
      >                                                                              
      >            iterate(`iterate')
       18.       
   691 .       * Add J-stat estimates from Hansen's J-test  
   692 .       estat overid 
       19.       estadd sca Jstat    = r(J)      , replace   : `estname' 
       20.       estadd sca Jdf      = r(J_df)   , replace   : `estname'  
       21.       estadd sca Jpval    = r(J_p)    , replace   : `estname'  
       22.           local converged_str = cond(e(converged)==1, "Y", "N")
       23.           estadd local converged_str "`converged_str'", replace : `estname
      > '
       24.           
   693 .           * Save results
   694 .       estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'", replace
       25.       
   695 .       * Compute Delta_never
   696 .       nlcom (Delta_never: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * ///
      >             (_b[mu:never] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post
       26.       * Save results
   697 .       estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_n", replace
       27.            
   698 .       * Compute Delta_always (average Delta for always-urban)
   699 .       estimates restore `estname'   // make sure the results are in memory
       28.       nlcom (Delta_always: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] *  ///
      >             (_b[kappa:_cons] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post           
       29.       * Save results
   700 .       estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_a", replace
       30.           
   701 .           * Compute Delta_avg (average Delta for all switchers)
   702 .           local first_loop = 1
       31.           local Delta_avg_nlcom ""
       32.           foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       33.                 estimates restore `estname'   // make sure the results are
      >  in memory
       34.         sum 1.switcher_`s' if e(sample)
       35.         local num_`s' = r(mean)   // proportion of sample in this trajecto
      > ry
       36.                 if `first_loop' == 0 {
       37.                         local Delta_avg_nlcom "`Delta_avg_nlcom' + (`num_`
      > s'' * (_b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:s
      > witcher_`base']))))"
       38.                 }
       39.                 else if `first_loop' == 1 {
       40.                         local Delta_avg_nlcom "(`num_`s'' * (_b[Delta_base
      > :_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))))"
       41.                         local first_loop = 0
       42.                 }
       43.       }
       44.           estimates restore `estname'   // make sure the results are in me
      > mory
       45.           nlcom (Delta_avg: `Delta_avg_nlcom'), post
       46.       * Save results
   703 .       estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_avg", replace
       47. 
   704 . end
      
   705 . 
   706 . * **********************************************************************
   707 . * run_grc_robust: Verdier (2020) Section F robust extrapolation
   708 . * **********************************************************************
   709 . * Implements the within-v demeaning estimator as a single-step
   710 . * extension of run_grc: adds |V|-1 cluster dummies x choice to the
   711 . * GMM equation so Delta_{d_0} becomes cluster-specific (beta(v)).
   712 . * See docs/reviews/2026-04-23_robust-grc-derivation.md for the full
   713 . * derivation. Standard errors switch from vce(cluster pid) to
   714 . * vce(cluster vfirst). The always-urban term uses scalar kappa
   715 . * (cross-origin extrapolation) per memo section 6.
   716 . * P1 aggregations: _never uses the cluster-share-weighted aggregate
   717 . * from memo section 7 (weights = never-urban counts per support
   718 . * cluster); _delta, _avg, _always use baseline-cluster beta
   719 . * (= Delta_base) as a placeholder -- proper cross-cluster aggregation
   720 . * deferred to P2.
   721 . capture program drop run_grc_robust
      
   722 . program define run_grc_robust
        1. 
   723 .     syntax , estname(string) switchers(numlist) base(numlist) balance(string)
      >  ///
      >         vindex(varname) ///
      >         [covars(varlist) iterate(numlist) initial(string) phistart(real -1)]
        2. 
   724 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   725 .     * Build vfirst + vchoice_* (idempotent; initial_values_robust may
   726 .     * have already built them and dropped missing-vfirst obs)
   727 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   728 .     gen_vfirst, vname(`vindex') genname(vfirst)
        3.     qui count if missing(vfirst)
        4.     if r(N) > 0 {
        5.         di as text "run_grc_robust: dropping " r(N) " obs with missing vfi
      > rst"
        6.     }
        7.     qui drop if missing(vfirst)
        8. 
   729 .     qui levelsof vfirst, local(vvals)
        9.     local V : word count `vvals'
       10.     local v_base : word 1 of `vvals'
       11. 
   730 .     local vchoice_list ""
       12.     foreach v of local vvals {
       13.         if `v' != `v_base' {
       14.             capture drop vchoice_`v'
       15.             qui gen vchoice_`v' = (vfirst == `v') * choice
       16.             local vchoice_list "`vchoice_list' vchoice_`v'"
       17.         }
       18.     }
       19. 
   731 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   732 .     * Cluster-support diagnostics (per plan section 3.1 step 2)
   733 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   734 .     tempvar first_obs n_sw_v n_nev_v n_tot_v
       20.     qui bysort pid (year): gen byte `first_obs' = (_n == 1)
       21.     qui bysort vfirst: egen `n_sw_v'  = sum(switcher * `first_obs')
       22.     qui bysort vfirst: egen `n_nev_v' = sum(never    * `first_obs')
       23.     qui bysort vfirst: egen `n_tot_v' = sum(`first_obs')
       24. 
   735 .     di as txt _newline(1) "run_grc_robust: cluster-support diagnostics"
       25.     di as txt "  |V| = `V' clusters (baseline v = `v_base')"
       26. 
   736 .     preserve
       27.         qui duplicates drop vfirst, force
       28.         qui sum `n_sw_v', detail
       29.         di as txt "  Switchers per cluster: mean=" %6.2f r(mean) ///
      >             ", p50=" %6.0f r(p50) ", max=" %6.0f r(max)
       30.         qui count if `n_sw_v' >= 10
       31.         local nclust_ge10 = r(N)
       32.         qui count if `n_sw_v' > 0 & `n_nev_v' > 0
       33.         local V_supp = r(N)
       34.         qui sum `n_nev_v' if `n_sw_v' > 0 & `n_nev_v' > 0
       35.         local nev_supp = r(sum)
       36.         qui sum `n_nev_v'
       37.         local nev_tot = r(sum)
       38.     restore
       39. 
   737 .     di as txt "  Clusters with >=10 switchers: `nclust_ge10' / `V'"
       40.     di as txt "  Clusters with both sw & never: `V_supp' / `V'"
       41.     if `nev_tot' > 0 {
       42.         di as txt "  Always-rural support share: " ///
      >             %5.1f (100 * `nev_supp' / `nev_tot') "%"
       43.     }
       44. 
   738 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   739 .     * Build covarlist and switcher_traj (same as run_grc)
   740 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   741 .     if "`balance'" == "unb" {
       45.         local covarlist "`covars' unbalanced unbalanced_choice"
       46.     }
       47.     else {
       48.         capture drop covar_cons
       49.         gen covar_cons = 0
       50.         local covarlist "`covars' covar_cons"
       51.     }
       52. 
   742 .     local switcher_traj
       53.     foreach s of numlist `switchers' {
       54.         local switcher_traj "`switcher_traj' switcher_`s'"
       55.     }
       56. 
   743 .     define_switcherpars, switchers(`switchers') base(`base')
       57.     local switcherpars `r(switcherpars)'
       58.     di as text "run_grc_robust: base trajectory = `base'"
       59.     di as text "run_grc_robust: |V|-1 cluster deviations = " ///
      >         `: word count `vchoice_list''
       60. 
   744 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   745 .     * GMM estimation
   746 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   747 .     * Degenerate V=1 branch: vchoice_list empty, equation reduces
   748 .     * exactly to run_grc_onestep. Used by the P1 degenerate-v test
   749 .     * (derivation memo section 8). vce switches to cluster pid
   750 .     * because vce(cluster vfirst) with 1 cluster yields undefined
   751 .     * cluster-robust variance (division by G-1=0). Uses the same
   752 .     * onestep + winitial settings as the non-degenerate branch so the
   753 .     * comparison to run_grc_onestep is apples-to-apples.
   754 .     di as text "run_grc_robust: phi initial value = `phistart'"
       61.     if "`vchoice_list'" == "" {
       62.         di as text "run_grc_robust: degenerate |V|=1 branch (no beta_dev p
      > arameters)"
       63.         di as text "run_grc_robust: switching to vce(cluster pid) for |V|=
      > 1"
       64.         eststo `estname': gmm (lndepvar - {mu: never `switcher_traj'}     
      >            ///
      >                                 - {Delta_base}*choice                        
      >         ///
      >                                 - {phi=`phistart'}*(`switcherpars')          
      >         ///
      >                                 - ({kappa}+{phi}*({kappa}                    
      >         ///
      >                                 - {mu: switcher_`base'}))*(always#1.choice)  
      >         ///
      >                                 - {xb: `covarlist'})                         
      >         ///
      >                                , instruments(                                
      >         ///
      >                                 `covarlist'                                  
      >         ///
      >                                 never `switcher_traj' choice                 
      >         ///
      >                                 always_choice switcher_*_choice, nocons      
      >         ///
      >                                )                                             
      >         ///
      >                                  vce(cluster pid)                            
      >         ///
      >                                  winitial(unadjusted, independent)           
      >         ///
      >                                  onestep                                     
      >         ///
      >                                  from(`initial')                             
      >         ///
      >                                  quickderivatives nolog                      
      >         ///
      >                                  iterate(`iterate')
       65.     }
       66.     else {
       67.         * Q8 (2026-04-23): adopt VV robust.do's GMM settings --
   755 .         * winitial(unadjusted, independent) onestep. VV does one-step
   756 .         * GMM with independence-weighted initial matrix; we match so
   757 .         * the second-step cluster-robust weighting matrix (which is
   758 .         * rank-deficient when #moments > #clusters, typical here --
   759 .         * 47 moments vs 26 TZA clusters) is never computed. This
   760 .         * resolves the non-convergence + singular-SE issues observed
   761 .         * under Stata default two-step GMM. Inference via
   762 .         * vce(cluster vfirst) still uses the cluster-robust formula.
   763 .         eststo `estname': gmm (lndepvar - {mu: never `switcher_traj'}        
      >         ///
      >                                 - {Delta_base}*choice                        
      >         ///
      >                                 - {beta_dev: `vchoice_list'}                 
      >         ///
      >                                 - {phi=`phistart'}*(`switcherpars')          
      >         ///
      >                                 - ({kappa}+{phi}*({kappa}                    
      >         ///
      >                                 - {mu: switcher_`base'}))*(always#1.choice)  
      >         ///
      >                                 - {xb: `covarlist'})                         
      >         ///
      >                                , instruments(                                
      >         ///
      >                                 `covarlist'                                  
      >         ///
      >                                 never `switcher_traj' choice `vchoice_list'  
      >         ///
      >                                 always_choice switcher_*_choice, nocons      
      >         ///
      >                                )                                             
      >         ///
      >                                  vce(cluster vfirst)                         
      >         ///
      >                                  winitial(unadjusted, independent)           
      >         ///
      >                                  onestep                                     
      >         ///
      >                                  from(`initial')                             
      >         ///
      >                                  quickderivatives nolog                      
      >         ///
      >                                  iterate(`iterate')
       68.     }
       69. 
   764 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   765 .     * Joint mu test + Hansen J (same as run_grc)
   766 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   767 .     local mu_test ""
       70.     local s0 : word 1 of $switchers
       71.     local mu_test "[mu]switcher_`s0'"
       72.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       73.         if `s' != `s0' {
       74.             local mu_test "`mu_test' = [mu]switcher_`s'"
       75.         }
       76.     }
       77.     test `mu_test'
       78.     estadd scalar joint_chi2 = r(chi2), replace : `estname'
       79.     estadd scalar joint_p    = r(p),    replace : `estname'
       80. 
   768 .     * Hansen's J: only available under twostep GMM. The non-degenerate
   769 .     * branch uses onestep (Q8 decision, matches VV), so estat overid
   770 .     * would error; capture and skip gracefully. Do-file must not abort
   771 .     * before log close / exit, STATA clear (avoids the Windows batch
   772 .     * "Stata finished" popup).
   773 .     capture estat overid
       81.     if _rc == 0 {
       82.         estadd sca Jstat = r(J),    replace : `estname'
       83.         estadd sca Jdf   = r(J_df), replace : `estname'
       84.         estadd sca Jpval = r(J_p),  replace : `estname'
       85.     }
       86.     else {
       87.         di as text "run_grc_robust: estat overid unavailable (onestep GMM)
      >  -- Jstat not computed"
       88.     }
       89.     local converged_str = cond(e(converged)==1, "Y", "N")
       90.     estadd local converged_str "`converged_str'", replace : `estname'
       91. 
   774 .     * Cluster diagnostics stored on the estimate for table builders
   775 .     estadd scalar V_clusters = `V',           replace : `estname'
       92.     estadd scalar V_ge10sw   = `nclust_ge10', replace : `estname'
       93.     estadd scalar V_supp     = `V_supp',      replace : `estname'
       94. 
   776 .     * Save main .ster
   777 .     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'", replace
       95. 
   778 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   779 .     * Delta_never: cluster-share-weighted aggregate over V_supp
   780 .     *   Delta_never = Delta_base + sum_{v != v_base, v in V_supp} w_v * beta_
      > dev_v
   781 .     *                            + phi * (mu:never - mu:switcher_base)
   782 .     *   w_v = n^N_v / sum_{v' in V_supp} n^N_{v'}
   783 .     * (Delta_base absorbs the baseline-cluster weight since weights sum to 1
   784 .     *  and beta_dev_{v_base} is 0 by the drop-a-dummy convention.)
   785 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   786 .     estimates restore `estname'
       96. 
   787 .     * Recompute support + per-cluster never counts (vchoice_list-indexed only
      > )
   788 .     preserve
       97.         qui duplicates drop vfirst, force
       98.         qui keep if `n_sw_v' > 0 & `n_nev_v' > 0
       99.         qui sum `n_nev_v'
      100.         local nev_supp_sum = r(sum)
      101. 
   789 .         * Build weighted sum of beta_dev terms
   790 .         local beta_agg_expr "_b[Delta_base:_cons]"
      102.         if `nev_supp_sum' > 0 & "`vchoice_list'" != "" {
      103.             forvalues i = 1/`=_N' {
      104.                 local v_i = vfirst[`i']
      105.                 local n_nev_i = `n_nev_v'[`i']
      106.                 if `v_i' != `v_base' {
      107.                     local w_i = `n_nev_i' / `nev_supp_sum'
      108.                     local beta_agg_expr "`beta_agg_expr' + `w_i' * _b[beta
      > _dev:vchoice_`v_i']"
      109.                 }
      110.             }
      111.         }
      112.     restore
      113. 
   791 .     di as text "run_grc_robust: Delta_never aggregate expression:"
      114.     di as text "  `beta_agg_expr' + phi * (mu_never - mu_base)"
      115. 
   792 .     nlcom (Delta_never: `beta_agg_expr' + _b[phi:_cons] * ///
      >             (_b[mu:never] - _b[mu:switcher_`base'])), post
      116.     estadd scalar V_never_supp = `V_supp', replace
      117.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_never", replace
      118. 
   793 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   794 .     * Delta_always (baseline-cluster beta; proper aggregation in P2)
   795 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   796 .     estimates restore `estname'
      119.     nlcom (Delta_always: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * ///
      >             (_b[kappa:_cons] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post
      120.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_always", replace
      121. 
   797 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   798 .     * Per-switcher Deltas (baseline-cluster beta) + joint test
   799 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   800 .     estimates restore `estname'
      122.     local nlcom_expr ""
      123.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
      124.         local nlcom_expr "`nlcom_expr' (Delta_`s': _b[Delta_base:_cons] + 
      > (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base'])))"
      125.     }
      126.     nlcom `nlcom_expr', post
      127. 
   801 .     local d_test ""
      128.     local s0 : word 1 of $switchers
      129.     local d_test "Delta_`s0'"
      130.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
      131.         if `s' != `s0' {
      132.             local d_test "`d_test' = Delta_`s'"
      133.         }
      134.     }
      135.     test `d_test'
      136.     estadd scalar joint_chi2 = r(chi2), replace
      137.     estadd scalar joint_p    = r(p),    replace
      138.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_delta", replace
      139. 
   802 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   803 .     * Delta_avg: trajectory-share-weighted average across switchers
   804 .     * (baseline-cluster beta; mirror run_grc structure)
   805 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   806 .     local first_loop = 1
      140.     local Delta_avg_nlcom ""
      141.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
      142.         estimates restore `estname'
      143.         sum 1.switcher_`s' if e(sample)
      144.         local num_`s' = r(mean)
      145.         if `first_loop' == 0 {
      146.             local Delta_avg_nlcom "`Delta_avg_nlcom' + (`num_`s'' * (_b[De
      > lta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`bas
      > e']))))"
      147.         }
      148.         else if `first_loop' == 1 {
      149.             local Delta_avg_nlcom "(`num_`s'' * (_b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_
      > b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))))"
      150.             local first_loop = 0
      151.         }
      152.     }
      153.     estimates restore `estname'
      154.     nlcom (Delta_avg: `Delta_avg_nlcom'), post
      155.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_avg", replace
      156. 
   807 . end
      
   808 . 
   809 . * **********************************************************************
   810 . * run_grc_robust_vv: VV-style robust extrapolation via cluster-demeaned
   811 . *                    instruments (no cluster-FE parameters)
   812 . * **********************************************************************
   813 . * Ports the core identification idea of Verdier (2020) Online Appendix
   814 . * Section F / Table1/Code/robust.do to CKT's trajectory-pooled GRC.
   815 . *
   816 . * Key difference vs run_grc_robust (single-step cluster-dummy version):
   817 . *   - run_grc_robust adds |V|-1 cluster*choice interactions as free
   818 . *     parameters (beta_dev_v). Asymptotically equivalent to VV's
   819 . *     approach, but in finite sample with few clusters the extra |V|-1
   820 . *     parameters make the GMM objective nearly flat in phi, giving
   821 . *     multiple local minima (confirmed on TZA; hung on CHN).
   822 . *   - run_grc_robust_vv keeps CKT's original parameters (phi, mu_d,
   823 . *     Delta_base, kappa, xb gammas) -- no cluster FEs -- and instead
   824 . *     replaces the switcher_s_choice instruments with their within-
   825 . *     cluster-demeaned residuals (regressed on i.vfirst among workers
   826 . *     in trajectory s). The within-cluster variation in treatment
   827 . *     identifies phi robustly to cluster-level confounders in the
   828 . *     outcome, matching VV's Prop. F identification assumption.
   829 . *
   830 . * This mirrors VV's Table1/Code/robust.do: he demeans hybrid_per on
   831 . * i.vil among switchers (line 12), uses the residual as the optimal
   832 . * instrument (line 14), and fits GMM with vce(cluster vil), winitial
   833 . * unadjusted independent, onestep (line 206). We follow the same
   834 . * pattern but adapted to CKT's trajectory structure.
   835 . *
   836 . * Aggregations for _never etc. follow VV Eq. F.3 conceptually -- for
   837 . * P1 we defer the full cluster-share-weighted aggregator to P2 and use
   838 . * the simple-spec extrapolation with VV-style phi.
   839 . capture program drop run_grc_robust_vv
      
   840 . program define run_grc_robust_vv
        1. 
   841 .     syntax , estname(string) switchers(numlist) base(numlist) balance(string)
      >  ///
      >         vindex(varname) ///
      >         [covars(varlist) iterate(numlist) initial(string) phistart(real -1)]
        2. 
   842 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   843 .     * Build vfirst + drop missing-vfirst obs
   844 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   845 .     gen_vfirst, vname(`vindex') genname(vfirst)
        3.     qui count if missing(vfirst)
        4.     if r(N) > 0 {
        5.         di as text "run_grc_robust_vv: dropping " r(N) " obs with missing 
      > vfirst"
        6.     }
        7.     qui drop if missing(vfirst)
        8. 
   846 .     qui levelsof vfirst, local(vvals)
        9.     local V : word count `vvals'
       10.     di as text "run_grc_robust_vv: |V| = `V' clusters"
       11. 
   847 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   848 .     * VV's first-stage optimal instrument construction:
   849 .     * for each switcher trajectory s, demean switcher_s_choice on
   850 .     * i.vfirst AMONG workers in trajectory s; residual is the within-
   851 .     * cluster treatment variation that identifies phi robustly.
   852 .     * Zero-fill for non-trajectory-s workers so the moment is defined
   853 .     * over the whole sample (contributes zero from non-switchers).
   854 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   855 .     local swd_list ""
       12.     foreach s of numlist `switchers' {
       13.         capture drop swd_switcher_`s'_choice
       14. 
   856 .         tempvar tmpy tmpresid
       15.         qui gen `tmpy' = switcher_`s'_choice if switcher_`s' == 1
       16.         qui reg `tmpy' i.vfirst if switcher_`s' == 1
       17.         qui predict `tmpresid' if switcher_`s' == 1, resid
       18. 
   857 .         qui gen swd_switcher_`s'_choice = `tmpresid'
       19.         qui replace swd_switcher_`s'_choice = 0 if missing(swd_switcher_`s
      > '_choice)
       20.         local swd_list "`swd_list' swd_switcher_`s'_choice"
       21.     }
       22. 
   858 .     * Demean always_choice on i.vfirst among always-urban workers.
   859 .     * Always-urban have choice=1 in every period so within-person
   860 .     * variation is zero; BUT cross-person variation in always-urban
   861 .     * status within a cluster contributes. Same pattern as VV.
   862 .     capture drop swd_always_choice
       23.     tempvar tmpy tmpresid
       24.     qui gen `tmpy' = always_choice if always == 1
       25.     qui reg `tmpy' i.vfirst if always == 1
       26.     qui predict `tmpresid' if always == 1, resid
       27.     qui gen swd_always_choice = `tmpresid'
       28.     qui replace swd_always_choice = 0 if missing(swd_always_choice)
       29. 
   863 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   864 .     * Cluster-support diagnostics (brief)
   865 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   866 .     tempvar first_obs n_sw_v
       30.     qui bysort pid (year): gen byte `first_obs' = (_n == 1)
       31.     qui bysort vfirst: egen `n_sw_v' = sum(switcher * `first_obs')
       32. 
   867 .     preserve
       33.         qui duplicates drop vfirst, force
       34.         qui sum `n_sw_v', detail
       35.         di as txt "run_grc_robust_vv: switchers/cluster mean=" ///
      >             %6.2f r(mean) " p50=" %6.0f r(p50) " max=" %6.0f r(max)
       36.         qui count if `n_sw_v' >= 10
       37.         local nclust_ge10 = r(N)
       38.     restore
       39.     di as txt "run_grc_robust_vv: clusters >=10 sw = `nclust_ge10' / `V'"
       40. 
   868 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   869 .     * Build covarlist + switcher_traj (same as run_grc)
   870 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   871 .     if "`balance'" == "unb" {
       41.         local covarlist "`covars' unbalanced unbalanced_choice"
       42.     }
       43.     else {
       44.         capture drop covar_cons
       45.         gen covar_cons = 0
       46.         local covarlist "`covars' covar_cons"
       47.     }
       48. 
   872 .     local switcher_traj
       49.     foreach s of numlist `switchers' {
       50.         local switcher_traj "`switcher_traj' switcher_`s'"
       51.     }
       52. 
   873 .     define_switcherpars, switchers(`switchers') base(`base')
       53.     local switcherpars `r(switcherpars)'
       54.     di as text "run_grc_robust_vv: base trajectory = `base'"
       55.     di as text "run_grc_robust_vv: phi initial value = `phistart'"
       56. 
   874 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   875 .     * GMM: same moment equation as run_grc, BUT instruments
   876 .     * swd_switcher_*_choice replace switcher_*_choice, and
   877 .     * swd_always_choice replaces always_choice. vce(cluster vfirst),
   878 .     * winitial unadjusted independent, onestep (VV's settings).
   879 .     * Parameter count identical to run_grc -- no beta_dev.
   880 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   881 .     eststo `estname': gmm (lndepvar - {mu: never `switcher_traj'}            
      >        ///
      >                             - {Delta_base}*choice                            
      >        ///
      >                             - {phi=`phistart'}*(`switcherpars')              
      >        ///
      >                             - ({kappa}+{phi}*({kappa}                        
      >        ///
      >                             - {mu: switcher_`base'}))*(always#1.choice)      
      >        ///
      >                             - {xb: `covarlist'})                             
      >        ///
      >                            , instruments(                                    
      >        ///
      >                             `covarlist'                                      
      >        ///
      >                             never `switcher_traj' choice                     
      >        ///
      >                             swd_always_choice `swd_list', nocons             
      >        ///
      >                            )                                                 
      >        ///
      >                              vce(cluster vfirst)                             
      >        ///
      >                              winitial(unadjusted, independent)               
      >        ///
      >                              onestep                                         
      >        ///
      >                              from(`initial')                                 
      >        ///
      >                              quickderivatives nolog                          
      >        ///
      >                              iterate(`iterate')
       57. 
   882 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   883 .     * Post-estimation (joint mu test, Hansen J, convergence flag)
   884 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   885 .     local mu_test ""
       58.     local s0 : word 1 of $switchers
       59.     local mu_test "[mu]switcher_`s0'"
       60.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       61.         if `s' != `s0' {
       62.             local mu_test "`mu_test' = [mu]switcher_`s'"
       63.         }
       64.     }
       65.     test `mu_test'
       66.     estadd scalar joint_chi2 = r(chi2), replace : `estname'
       67.     estadd scalar joint_p    = r(p),    replace : `estname'
       68. 
   886 .     capture estat overid
       69.     if _rc == 0 {
       70.         estadd sca Jstat = r(J),    replace : `estname'
       71.         estadd sca Jdf   = r(J_df), replace : `estname'
       72.         estadd sca Jpval = r(J_p),  replace : `estname'
       73.     }
       74.     else {
       75.         di as text "run_grc_robust_vv: estat overid unavailable (onestep G
      > MM) -- Jstat not computed"
       76.     }
       77.     local converged_str = cond(e(converged)==1, "Y", "N")
       78.     estadd local converged_str "`converged_str'", replace : `estname'
       79.     estadd scalar V_clusters = `V',           replace : `estname'
       80.     estadd scalar V_ge10sw   = `nclust_ge10', replace : `estname'
       81. 
   887 .     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'", replace
       82. 
   888 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   889 .     * Standard nlcoms (Delta_never, Delta_always, per-switcher Delta,
   890 .     * Delta_avg). Identical structure to run_grc (phi is now
   891 .     * VV-robust; the LCA extrapolation formulas are unchanged).
   892 .     * ----------------------------------------------------------------
   893 .     estimates restore `estname'
       83.     nlcom (Delta_never: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * ///
      >             (_b[mu:never] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post
       84.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_never", replace
       85. 
   894 .     estimates restore `estname'
       86.     nlcom (Delta_always: _b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] *  ///
      >             (_b[kappa:_cons] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))), post
       87.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_always", replace
       88. 
   895 .     estimates restore `estname'
       89.     local nlcom_expr ""
       90.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       91.         local nlcom_expr "`nlcom_expr' (Delta_`s': _b[Delta_base:_cons] + 
      > (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base'])))"
       92.     }
       93.     nlcom `nlcom_expr', post
       94. 
   896 .     local d_test ""
       95.     local s0 : word 1 of $switchers
       96.     local d_test "Delta_`s0'"
       97.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
       98.         if `s' != `s0' {
       99.             local d_test "`d_test' = Delta_`s'"
      100.         }
      101.     }
      102.     test `d_test'
      103.     estadd scalar joint_chi2 = r(chi2), replace
      104.     estadd scalar joint_p    = r(p),    replace
      105.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_delta", replace
      106. 
   897 .     local first_loop = 1
      107.     local Delta_avg_nlcom ""
      108.     foreach s of numlist $switchers {
      109.         estimates restore `estname'
      110.         sum 1.switcher_`s' if e(sample)
      111.         local num_`s' = r(mean)
      112.         if `first_loop' == 0 {
      113.             local Delta_avg_nlcom "`Delta_avg_nlcom' + (`num_`s'' * (_b[De
      > lta_base:_cons] + (_b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`bas
      > e']))))"
      114.         }
      115.         else if `first_loop' == 1 {
      116.             local Delta_avg_nlcom "(`num_`s'' * (_b[Delta_base:_cons] + (_
      > b[phi:_cons] * (_b[mu:switcher_`s'] - _b[mu:switcher_`base']))))"
      117.             local first_loop = 0
      118.         }
      119.     }
      120.     estimates restore `estname'
      121.     nlcom (Delta_avg: `Delta_avg_nlcom'), post
      122.     estimates save "$dir/output/`estname'_avg", replace
      123. 
   898 . end
      
   899 . 
   900 . * **********************************************************************
   901 . * Create country-specific LaTeX table with GRC results
   902 . * **********************************************************************
   903 . cap program drop grc_tex_table
      
   904 . program define grc_tex_table
        1.     syntax , COLumns(integer) FILEname(string asis)     ///
      >                                 COUNTRY(string asis) KEEP(string) varlabel(st
      > ring asis)         ///
      >                                 PREhead(string asis) POSTfoot(string asis) //
      > /
      >                                 COEFLABels(string asis) TEXTdepvar(string asi
      > s)
        2.         
   905 .     // Split the panel names, prehead, and postfoot strings into tokens
   906 . 
   907 .     local num_panels `panels'
        3.     local ccc ""
        4.     * Loop to concatenate "c" the number of times specified in `columns'
   908 .     forval i = 1/`columns' {
        5.         local ccc "`ccc'c"
        6.     }
        7.     local cmid = `columns' + 1
        8.                 local colnumbers ""
        9.                 local table_prehead     ""
       10.                 local table_postfoot    ""
       11.                 local posthead                  ""
       12.     local table_prehead1 "`"\begin{table}[htbp] \centering \begin{threepar
      > ttable}"'"
       13.     local table_prehead2 "`"\begin{tabular}{l `ccc'} \toprule  \textbf{Dep
      > . var:} `textdepvar'"'"
       14.     local table_prehead "`table_prehead1' `prehead' `table_prehead2'"
       15.                 local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2-`cmid'} `postfoot'"
       16. 
   909 .     * Empty locals to store estimates
   910 .     local ests_never = "" 
       17.         local ests_avg = ""
       18.     local ests = ""       
       19.                 
   911 .     * Generate the list of stored estimates for the current panel
   912 .       foreach estname in covs_0 covs_1 covs_2 covs_all covs_trend {
       20.         local ests_never  = "`ests_never' grc_`country'_`estname'_never"
       21.                 local ests_avg = "`ests_avg' grc_`country'_`estname'_avg"
       22.         local ests        = "`ests' grc_`country'_`estname'"
       23.       }
       24.         
   913 .       * Output Delta-never row
   914 .       esttab `ests_never'                    ///
      >       using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >           se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >       fragment booktabs noobs                ///
      >       collabels("")                          ///
      >       starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >       varwidth(20)                               ///
      >       nolines nomtitles `colnumbers'         ///
      >       prehead(`table_prehead')               ///
      >       posthead(`table_posthead')             ///
      >       coeflabels(Delta_never "$\Delta_{\text{never}}$" Delta_always "$\Delta_
      > {\text{always}}$") ///
      >       replace substitute(\_ _)
       25.         
   915 .       * Output Delta average row
   916 .       esttab `ests_avg'                              ///
      >       using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >           se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >       fragment booktabs noobs                ///
      >       collabels("")                          ///
      >       starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >       varwidth(20)                               ///
      >       nolines nomtitles nonum                    ///
      >       coeflabels(Delta_avg "Average $\Delta$") ///
      >       append substitute(\_ _)
       26.     
   917 .     * Output other estimates
   918 .       esttab `ests'                              ///
      >       using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >           se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >       keep(`keep')                           ///
      >       fragment booktabs                      ///
      >       collabels("")                          ///
      >       starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >       s(N_clust N Jstat Jpval converged_str, label( "Individuals" "Observatio
      > ns" "J-stat" "J-stat (p-value)" "Converged") ///
      >       fmt(%9.0fc %9.0fc %8.1fc %8.3fc %8.0fc))      ///
      >       varwidth(20)                           ///
      >       nolines nomtitles nonum                ///
      >       postfoot("`table_postfoot'")           ///
      >           varlabels(`keep' "`varlabel'")         ///
      >       append substitute(\_ _)
       27.    
   919 . end
      
   920 . 
   921 . * **********************************************************************
   922 . * Create country-specific LaTeX table with GRC results 
   923 . *       ==> FOR TREND FIRST DO FILE
   924 . * **********************************************************************
   925 . cap program drop grc_tex_table_trend
      
   926 . program define grc_tex_table_trend
        1.     syntax , COLumns(integer) FILEname(string asis)     ///
      >                                 COUNTRY(string asis) KEEP(string) varlabel(st
      > ring)      ///
      >                                 htb(string) PREhead(string asis) POSTfoot(str
      > ing asis) ///
      >                                 COEFLABels(string asis) TEXTdepvar(string asi
      > s)
        2.         
   927 .     // Split the panel names, prehead, and postfoot strings into tokens
   928 . 
   929 .     local num_panels `panels'
        3.     local ccc ""
        4.     * Loop to concatenate "c" the number of times specified in `columns'
   930 .     forval i = 1/`columns' {
        5.         local ccc "`ccc'c"
        6.     }
        7.     local cmid = `columns' + 1
        8.                 local colnumbers ""
        9.                 local table_prehead     ""
       10.                 local table_postfoot    ""
       11.                 local posthead                  ""
       12.     local table_prehead1 "`"\begin{table}[`htb'] \centering \begin{threepa
      > rttable}"'"
       13.     local table_prehead2 "`"\begin{tabular}{l `ccc'} \toprule  \textbf{Dep
      > . var:} `textdepvar'"'"
       14.     local table_prehead "`table_prehead1' `prehead' `table_prehead2'"
       15.                 local table_postfoot "\cmidrule{2-`cmid'} `postfoot'"
       16. 
   931 .     * Empty locals to store estimates
   932 .     local ests_never = ""
       17.         local ests_avg = ""
       18.     local ests = ""       
       19.                 
   933 .     * Generate the list of stored estimates for the current panel
   934 .       foreach estname in covs_0 covs_trend covs_1 covs_2 covs_all {
       20.         local ests_never     = "`ests_never' grc_`country'_`estname'_never
      > "
       21.         local ests_avg = "`ests_avg' grc_`country'_`estname'_avg"
       22.         local ests           = "`ests' grc_`country'_`estname'"
       23.       }
       24.         
   935 .       * Output Delta-never row
   936 .       esttab `ests_never'                    ///
      >       using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >           se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >       fragment booktabs noobs                ///
      >       collabels("")                          ///
      >       starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >       varwidth(20)                               ///
      >       nolines nomtitles `colnumbers'         ///
      >       prehead(`table_prehead')               ///
      >       posthead(`table_posthead')             ///
      >       coeflabels(Delta_never "$\Delta_{\text{never}}$" Delta_always "$\Delta_
      > {\text{always}}$") ///
      >       replace substitute(\_ _)
       25.         
   937 .       * Output Delta average row
   938 .       esttab `ests_avg'                              ///
      >       using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >           se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >       fragment booktabs noobs                ///
      >       collabels("")                          ///
      >       starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >       varwidth(20)                               ///
      >       nolines nomtitles nonum                    ///
      >       coeflabels(Delta_avg "Average $\Delta$") ///
      >       append substitute(\_ _)
       26.     
   939 .     * Output other estimates
   940 .       esttab `ests'                              ///
      >       using "$output/tables/`filename'.tex", ///
      >           se b(%8.3f)                            ///           
      >       keep(`keep')                           ///
      >       varlabels(`keep' "`varlabel'")         ///
      >       eqlabels(none)                                     ///
      >       fragment booktabs                      ///
      >       collabels("")                          ///
      >       starlevels(* 0.10 ** 0.05 *** 0.01)    ///
      >       s(N_clust N Jstat Jpval converged_str, label( "Individuals" "Observatio
      > ns" "J-stat" "J-stat (p-value)" "Converged") ///
      >       fmt(%9.0fc %9.0fc %8.1fc %8.3fc %8.0fc))      ///
      >       varwidth(20)                           ///
      >       nolines nomtitles nonum                ///
      >       postfoot("`table_postfoot'")           ///
      >       append substitute(\_ _)
       27.    
   941 . end

  1036 . 
  1037 . 
  1038 . * ============================================================
  1039 . * MC parameters
  1040 . * ============================================================
  1041 . capture noisily {
  1042 . 
  1043 .     local n_reps      100
  1044 .     local N           4000
  1045 .     local T           4
  1046 .     local V           25
  1047 .     local phi_true    -0.7
  1048 .     local sigma_theta 0.5
  1049 .     local sigma_xi    0.3
  1050 .     local sigma_u     0.3
  1051 .     local sigma_beta  0.4
  1052 .     local c1          1.0
  1053 .     local c2          0.5
  1054 .     local m_slope     0.1
  1055 .     local lambda_r2a  1.0
  1056 .     local lambda_r2b  1.0
  1057 .     local seed_base   20260425
  1058 . 
  1059 .     local results_dta "x_equivalence_simulation_results.dta"
  1060 .     capture rm "`results_dta'"
  1061 . 
  1062 .     tempname pf
  1063 .     postfile `pf' str4 regime int rep                         ///
      >         double phi_ckt se_ckt phi_vv_inv se_vv_inv            ///
      >         double tv_realized n_switchers ckt_ok vv_ok           ///
      >         using "`results_dta'", replace
      (file x_equivalence_simulation_results.dta not found)
  1064 . 
  1065 .     foreach regime in "R1" "R2a" "R2b" {
        2. 
  1066 .         di as result _newline(2) "######### regime = `regime' #########"
        3. 
  1067 .         * Per-regime seed offset so different regimes don't share rep seeds
  1068 .         if "`regime'" == "R1"  local seed_offset 0
        4.         if "`regime'" == "R2a" local seed_offset 100000
        5.         if "`regime'" == "R2b" local seed_offset 200000
        6. 
  1069 .         forvalues r = 1/`n_reps' {
        7. 
  1070 .             local seed = `seed_base' + `seed_offset' + `r'
        8.             di as result _newline "--- regime=`regime' rep=`r' seed=`seed'
      >  ---"
        9. 
  1071 .             local ckt_ok = 0
       10.             local vv_ok  = 0
       11.             local phi_ckt = .
       12.             local se_ckt  = .
       13.             local phi_vv_inv = .
       14.             local se_vv_inv  = .
       15.             local tv_realized = .
       16.             local n_sw_rep = .
       17. 
  1072 .             capture noisily {
       18. 
  1073 .                 clear
       19.                 set seed `seed'
       20. 
  1074 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1075 .                 * DGP: trajectory and (regime-dependent) cluster assignment
  1076 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1077 .                 set obs `N'
       21.                 gen long pid = _n
       22. 
  1078 .                 * Trajectory: uniform 1..16. Trajectory 1=never (RRRR),
  1079 .                 * 16=always (UUUU). Switchers are 2..15.
  1080 .                 gen int trajectory = 1 + floor(16 * runiform())
       23. 
  1081 .                 * Cluster assignment
  1082 .                 if "`regime'" == "R2a" {
       24.                     * Mode B: P(v|s) propto 1 + lambda*g(s)*h(v).
  1083 .                     * Discrete inverse-CDF sampling per pid.
  1084 .                     gen double g_s = (trajectory - 8.5) / 7.5
       25.                     gen byte vfirst_true = .
       26.                     forvalues v = 1/`V' {
       27.                         local h_v = (`v' - (`V'+1)/2) / ((`V'-1)/2)
       28.                         gen double w_v_`v' = (1 + `lambda_r2a' * g_s * `h_
      > v') / `V'
       29.                     }
       30.                     * Cumulative sums and uniform draw
  1085 .                     gen double w_cum_0 = 0
       31.                     forvalues v = 1/`V' {
       32.                         local vm1 = `v' - 1
       33.                         gen double w_cum_`v' = w_cum_`vm1' + w_v_`v'
       34.                     }
       35.                     gen double u_v = runiform()
       36.                     forvalues v = 1/`V' {
       37.                         local vm1 = `v' - 1
       38.                         replace vfirst_true = `v' if missing(vfirst_true) 
      > ///
      >                             & u_v > w_cum_`vm1' & u_v <= w_cum_`v'
       39.                     }
       40.                     * Edge case: u rounding may leave vfirst missing
  1086 .                     replace vfirst_true = `V' if missing(vfirst_true)
       41.                     drop g_s w_v_* w_cum_* u_v
       42.                 }
       43.                 else {
       44.                     * R1, R2b: uniform cluster
  1087 .                     gen byte vfirst_true = 1 + floor(`V' * runiform())
       45.                 }
       46. 
  1088 .                 * h(v) and m(s)
  1089 .                 gen double h_v = (vfirst_true - (`V'+1)/2) / ((`V'-1)/2)
       47.                 gen double m_s = `m_slope' * (trajectory - 8.5)
       48. 
  1090 .                 * theta_i: regime-dependent
  1091 .                 if "`regime'" == "R2b" {
       49.                     * Mode A: theta tilted by lambda*g(s)*h(v)
  1092 .                     gen double g_s = (trajectory - 8.5) / 7.5
       50.                     gen double theta_i = m_s + `lambda_r2b' * g_s * h_v //
      > /
      >                         + `sigma_theta' * rnormal()
       51.                     drop g_s
       52.                 }
       53.                 else {
       54.                     gen double theta_i = m_s + `sigma_theta' * rnormal()
       55.                 }
       56. 
  1093 .                 * beta(v) = sigma_beta * h(v); alpha_i = c1*theta + c2*eta
  1094 .                 gen double beta_v  = `sigma_beta' * h_v
       57.                 gen double eta_i   = rnormal()
       58.                 gen double alpha_i = `c1' * theta_i + `c2' * eta_i
       59.                 gen double xi_i    = `sigma_xi' * rnormal()
       60.                 gen double Delta_i = beta_v + `phi_true' * theta_i + xi_i
       61. 
  1095 .                 * Expand to panel
  1096 .                 expand `T'
       62.                 bysort pid: gen byte t = _n
       63.                 gen int period = t
       64.                 gen int year = 2000 + t
       65. 
  1097 .                 * D_it = bit_(T-t+1) of (trajectory - 1), MSB to LSB
  1098 .                 gen byte D_it = mod(floor((trajectory - 1) / 2^(`T' - t)), 2)
       66.                 rename D_it choice
       67. 
  1099 .                 * Outcome
  1100 .                 gen double u_it = `sigma_u' * rnormal()
       68.                 gen double lndepvar = alpha_i + Delta_i * choice + u_it
       69. 
  1101 .                 * Setup placeholders for setup_grc_estimation
  1102 .                 gen byte female = 0
       70.                 gen byte age = 40
       71.                 gen double age2 = age^2
       72.                 gen byte education_max = 0
       73.                 gen double education_max2 = education_max^2
       74.                 gen byte unbalanced = 0
       75.                 gen byte unbalanced_choice = 0
       76. 
  1103 .                 * vfirst lookup variable
  1104 .                 gen int prov = vfirst_true
       77. 
  1105 .                 sort pid period
       78.                 xtset pid period
       79. 
  1106 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1107 .                 * CKT pipeline setup
  1108 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1109 .                 setup_grc_estimation
       80. 
  1110 .                 qui sum trajectory
       81.                 local maxtraj = r(max)
       82.                 capture drop switcher non_switcher pid_first_obs
       83.                 gen byte switcher     = (trajectory > 1 & trajectory < `ma
      > xtraj')
       84.                 gen byte non_switcher = (trajectory == 1 | trajectory == `
      > maxtraj')
       85.                 bysort pid (year): gen byte pid_first_obs = (_n == 1)
       86. 
  1111 .                 * vfirst (override gen_vfirst since we have prov already)
  1112 .                 capture drop vfirst
       87.                 gen int vfirst = prov
       88. 
  1113 .                 * Switcher count this rep (worker-level)
  1114 .                 qui count if switcher == 1 & pid_first_obs == 1
       89.                 local n_sw_rep = r(N)
       90. 
  1115 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1116 .                 * Realized TV distance (R2a only -- diagnostic for cal)
  1117 .                 * Mean pairwise TV across switcher trajectories.
  1118 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1119 .                 if "`regime'" == "R2a" {
       91.                     preserve
       92.                         keep if switcher == 1 & pid_first_obs == 1
       93.                         qui tab trajectory
       94.                         local n_sw_traj = r(r)
       95.                         if `n_sw_traj' >= 2 {
       96.                             * Build P(v|s) matrix
  1120 .                             qui levelsof trajectory, local(slist)
       97.                             tempname Pmat
       98.                             mat `Pmat' = J(`n_sw_traj', `V', 0)
       99.                             local i_s = 0
      100.                             foreach s of local slist {
      101.                                 local i_s = `i_s' + 1
      102.                                 qui count if trajectory == `s'
      103.                                 local n_s = r(N)
      104.                                 forvalues v = 1/`V' {
      105.                                     qui count if trajectory == `s' & vfirs
      > t == `v'
      106.                                     mat `Pmat'[`i_s', `v'] = r(N) / `n_s'
      107.                                 }
      108.                             }
      109.                             * Mean pairwise TV
  1121 .                             local tv_sum = 0
      110.                             local n_pairs = 0
      111.                             forvalues a = 1/`n_sw_traj' {
      112.                                 forvalues b = `=`a'+1'/`n_sw_traj' {
      113.                                     local tv_ab = 0
      114.                                     forvalues v = 1/`V' {
      115.                                         local d_ab = `Pmat'[`a',`v'] - `Pm
      > at'[`b',`v']
      116.                                         if `d_ab' < 0 local d_ab = -`d_ab'
      117.                                         local tv_ab = `tv_ab' + `d_ab'
      118.                                     }
      119.                                     local tv_sum = `tv_sum' + 0.5 * `tv_ab
      > '
      120.                                     local n_pairs = `n_pairs' + 1
      121.                                 }
      122.                             }
      123.                             if `n_pairs' > 0 local tv_realized = `tv_sum' 
      > / `n_pairs'
      124.                         }
      125.                     restore
      126.                 }
      127.                 else {
      128.                     local tv_realized = 0
      129.                 }
      130. 
  1122 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1123 .                 * Estimator 1: CKT trajectory-pooled run_grc_robust_vv
  1124 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1125 .                 qui tab period, gen(period_)
      131.                 qui levelsof period, local(pers)
      132.                 local n_per : word count `pers'
      133.                 local periodFE "period_2 - period_`n_per'"
      134. 
  1126 .                 capture noisily {
      135.                     qui initial_values lndepvar,         ///
      >                         switchers($switchers)            ///
      >                         balance(unb)                     ///
      >                         estname(mc_init)
      136.                     local base    `r(base)'
      137.                     local initial "`r(initial)'"
      138. 
  1127 .                     qui run_grc_robust_vv,               ///
      >                         estname(mc_robust_vv)            ///
      >                         switchers($switchers)            ///
      >                         base(`base')                     ///
      >                         initial(`initial')               ///
      >                         balance(unb) vindex(prov)        ///
      >                         covars(`periodFE')               ///
      >                         iterate(500)
      139. 
  1128 .                     qui estimates use "$output/mc_robust_vv"
      140.                     local phi_ckt = _b[phi:_cons]
      141.                     local se_ckt  = _se[phi:_cons]
      142.                     local ckt_ok = 1
      143.                 }
      144. 
  1129 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1130 .                 * Estimator 2: VV worker-level Chamberlain + GMM
  1131 .                 * --------------------------------------------------------
  1132 .                 capture noisily {
      145.                     capture drop pid_choice
      146.                     gen long pid_choice = pid * 10 + choice
      147. 
  1133 .                     qui areg lndepvar period_2-period_`n_per', absorb(pid_cho
      > ice)
      148.                     capture drop d_hat
      149.                     qui predict d_hat if e(sample), d
      150. 
  1134 .                     capture drop a_pid apb_pid a apb return_pid
      151.                     bysort pid: egen a_pid   = mean(d_hat) if choice == 0
      152.                     bysort pid: egen apb_pid = mean(d_hat) if choice == 1
      153.                     bysort pid: egen a       = max(a_pid)
      154.                     bysort pid: egen apb     = max(apb_pid)
      155.                     drop a_pid apb_pid
      156.                     gen return_pid = apb - a
      157. 
  1135 .                     * Village-demeaned period-treatment instruments
  1136 .                     local instr_list ""
      158.                     forvalues p = 1/`n_per' {
      159.                         capture drop hybrid`p' hybrid`p'd _tmp_h
      160.                         gen _tmp_h = choice if period == `p'
      161.                         bysort pid: egen hybrid`p' = max(_tmp_h)
      162.                         drop _tmp_h
      163.                         qui reg hybrid`p' i.vfirst if switcher == 1
      164.                         qui predict hybrid`p'd if switcher == 1, resid
      165.                         qui replace hybrid`p'd = 0 if missing(hybrid`p'd)
      166.                         local instr_list "`instr_list' hybrid`p'd"
      167.                     }
      168. 
  1137 .                     preserve
      169.                         qui collapse (mean) a return_pid `instr_list' ///
      >                             (first) vfirst switcher, by(pid)
      170. 
  1138 .                         local instr_kept ""
      171.                         foreach inst of local instr_list {
      172.                             qui sum `inst' if switcher == 1
      173.                             if r(sd) > 1e-8 local instr_kept "`instr_kept'
      >  `inst'"
      174.                         }
      175. 
  1139 .                         capture noisily {
      176.                             qui gmm (a - {alpha0} - {alpha1}*return_pid), 
      >  ///
      >                                 instruments(`instr_kept')                  //
      > /
      >                                 vce(cluster vfirst)                        //
      > /
      >                                 winitial(unadjusted, independent)          //
      > /
      >                                 onestep                                    //
      > /
      >                                 quickderivatives nolog                     //
      > /
      >                                 iterate(500)
      177. 
  1140 .                             local alpha1 = _b[alpha1:_cons]
      178.                             local se_a1  = _se[alpha1:_cons]
      179.                             if !missing(`alpha1') & abs(`alpha1') > 1e-6 {
      180.                                 local phi_vv_inv = 1 / `alpha1'
      181.                                 local se_vv_inv  = `se_a1' / (`alpha1')^2
      182.                                 local vv_ok = 1
      183.                             }
      184.                         }
      185.                     restore
      186.                 }
      187. 
  1141 .                 di as result "rep=`r' regime=`regime' " ///
      >                     "ckt_phi=" %7.4f `phi_ckt' " (ok=`ckt_ok') " ///
      >                     "vv_phi=" %7.4f `phi_vv_inv' " (ok=`vv_ok') " ///
      >                     "tv=" %6.4f `tv_realized'
      188.             }
      189. 
  1142 .             post `pf' ("`regime'") (`r')                                    /
      > //
      >                 (cond(missing(`phi_ckt'), ., `phi_ckt'))                    /
      > //
      >                 (cond(missing(`se_ckt'), ., `se_ckt'))                      /
      > //
      >                 (cond(missing(`phi_vv_inv'), ., `phi_vv_inv'))              /
      > //
      >                 (cond(missing(`se_vv_inv'), ., `se_vv_inv'))                /
      > //
      >                 (cond(missing(`tv_realized'), ., `tv_realized'))            /
      > //
      >                 (cond(missing(`n_sw_rep'), ., `n_sw_rep'))                  /
      > //
      >                 (`ckt_ok') (`vv_ok')
      190.         }
      191.     }
